# Content trace: the United States and the Allied Industrial Base

Each claim of the brief with the dashboard field it comes from, the source datasets and the evidence quality it rests on.

**s1.1** In the Allied Industrial Base the United States is the demand and market anchor, with a core seat in all ten strategic compacts.

- `aib.n_compacts` = ten | AIB strategic.countries[].slots and items | Allied Industrial Base model: roles, compacts, trade restored and new capacity across the allied coalition, computed by the Explorer's authors | evidence: AIB model run

**s1.5** The fence that matters is narrow: the rare-earth chain, the electrical steel inside transformers, and the control software of the battery systems it imports mostly from China.

- `cells.batteries-assembly.coercive_top` = China | posture detail cells[] (need components and trade) | United Nations, UN Comtrade Database, HS6 bilateral trade (all reporters); reconciled by the authors | evidence: position evidence moderate; choice confidence high

**s1.9** The rare-earth chain from refining to traction magnets, where China holds 94% of world refining supply; grain-oriented electrical steel for transformers; and the firmware and communications of imported battery systems.

- `cells.electric-vehicles-refining.coercive_top` = China | posture detail cells[] (need components and trade) | United Nations, UN Comtrade Database, HS6 bilateral trade (all reporters); reconciled by the authors | evidence: position evidence strong; choice confidence moderate
- `cells.electric-vehicles-refining.coercive_top_share` = 94% | posture detail cells[] (need components and trade) | United Nations, UN Comtrade Database, HS6 bilateral trade (all reporters); reconciled by the authors | evidence: position evidence strong; choice confidence moderate

**s1.15** Source battery and EV minerals from Australia and Canada, where allied supply covers the need, and the wider wind-materials refining step from the allied holders Germany, Belgium and Spain.

- `cells.wind-refining.partners` = Germany, Belgium and Spain | posture detail cells[].partners, partner_aib_status, partner_gap, partner_evidence | Country strategy choices by technology and supply-chain step (supply-chain need, Strategic Industrial Value, Competitive Position, deployment need, policy alignment), computed by the Explorer's authors | Allied Industrial Base model: roles, compacts, trade restored and new capacity across the allied coalition, computed by the Explorer's authors | United Nations, UN Comtrade Database, HS6 bilateral trade (all reporters); reconciled by the authors | evidence: position evidence strong; choice confidence moderate; partner evidence strong

**s1.25** A corner flag marks a mixed step whose named parts sit behind a different fence: in battery cells and packs, the battery software is Secure while the cells and enclosures are Preserve.

**s2.10** The core steel of power and distribution transformers, on which every grid expansion depends; it sits inside grid metals refining, a step otherwise left open.

- `cells.electric-grid-refining.name` = grid metals refining | posture core meta.phrases / meta.stage_display | Country strategy choices by technology and supply-chain step (supply-chain need, Strategic Industrial Value, Competitive Position, deployment need, policy alignment), computed by the Explorer's authors | evidence: position evidence strong; choice confidence high

**s2.11** Essential, import-dependent and slow to rebuild, in a step whose supply-chain need is 91; the need is measured for grid metals refining as a whole, not for the steel alone.
